- [ ] 55. Network, clients and routing pages: no swallowed reads, no side channels, shared helpers
  What to do / Must NOT do: Per the findings: failed router reads become visible errors, not defaults that drive changes; replace the `Arc<Mutex<Option<String>>>` note side channel with a return value; dedupe helpers/constants; route read-back compares normalized values; AP rows read password presence from the typed model (W3-14's 'strip `authentication` from Interface' part is already done by todo 6/C-04); remove misleading/dead bits; fix the ambiguous test. Findings closed by this todo (fix each one; where a critic correction is given it overrides the reviewer's text):
  - `W3-05` (medium; critic adjusted) Failed router reads are swallowed into defaults that drive later changes. Evidence: see the finding text. Fix: On a dialog `GET`, answer with `net_change::unusable(..)` (already used by `net_internet_settings.rs:46`). In checks, return `Rejected::Failed(unread(..))` instead of defaulting. Critic correction (binding): 1. The two dialogs whose wrong defaults could plan a management change still ask before it runs: security level uses `.confirming(Risk::Management)` (`net_interface_edits.rs:201`), and the address dialog uses `.confirming_planned` (`net_internet_address.rs:193`). The risk is a misleading preselected value, not a change made without confirmation. 2. `frame.rs:256-263` logs `tracing::warn!`, so that site is downgraded, not silent. 3. The stronger case is `net_internet_create.rs:273-275`: with `taken` empty, the "exists" check is skipped and `plan_create` runs on an existing connection's name.
  - `W3-10` (low; critic confirmed) `Arc<Mutex<Option<String>>>` side channel copied three times to pass a note out of the work closure. Evidence: `clients_pages/segment_create.rs:42`, `segment_delete.rs:153-157`, `routing_page/route_remove.rs:167-169,215-219`. Fix: Have `Applied` (or the kit's `Outcome`) carry an optional extra note or report, and return it from the work instead of smuggling it through a mutex.
  - `W3-11` (low; critic adjusted) Same small helpers and constants duplicated across page families. Evidence: `firewall_page/checks.rs:29-37`, `nat_page/forward_forms.rs:187-195`, `routing_page/route_rows.rs:249-254`, `net_internet_address.rs:85-88`. Fix: Move the checks into one `page_kit` (or domain) helper, use the single `clients_pages::TabLink`, and take segment object names from the domain. Critic correction (binding): The cited `routing_page/route_rows.rs:249-254` is the shared `mask()` check, not a copy. The contiguity rule is copied at the other two sites only (the reviewer's citations, not re-checked by me), so a rule change touches up to three places, not three copies.
  - `W3-13` (low; critic confirmed) Static route read-back compares typed text with the router's stored text. Evidence: `routing_page/route_form.rs:36-57`, `route_add.rs:160`, `route_rows.rs:68-82`. Fix: Canonicalise in `check` (parse into `Ipv4Addr`/`Ipv6Addr` and network-mask them) before building `StaticRoute`, or compare parsed addresses.
  - `W3-14` (low; critic adjusted) Access-point rows round-trip through serde and read password presence from raw interface data. Evidence: `net_wifi_rows.rs:226-233`, `kcc-domain/src/interfaces/models.rs:9-37`, `wifi/models.rs:28-44`. Fix: Have the domain expose `psk_present: bool` on `AccessPoint` (or strip `authentication` in `Interface`'s deserialiser) and read access points via `wifi::read_access_points`. Critic correction (binding): The root cause is in `kcc_domain::interfaces::Interface`. The fix is a list reader for access points in `wifi`, plus stripping `authentication` from the domain `Interface` model.
  - `W3-15` (low; critic adjusted) Misleading or dead bits. Evidence: `net_pages.rs:8-9`, `clients_pages/dhcp.rs:173`, `routing_page/table_rows.rs:49-51`. Fix: Fix the doc path. Match `DhcpView::Arp` alone and make `Pools` `unreachable`-free by restructuring the match. Add an explicit `is_static: bool`. Critic correction (binding): Drop the DHCP-arm sub-item; keep the doc path and the stringly-typed `source`.
  - `W3-16` (low; critic adjusted) Ambiguous test label hides a missing case. Evidence: `tests/network_pages/internet.rs:238-249`. Fix: Rename the variable, and add a case that posts an existing PPPoE name and asserts `WEB_INTERNET_NAME_EXISTS`. Critic correction (binding): The code is at `tests/network_pages/internet.rs:128-145`.

- [ ] 57. Kit and config page leftovers
  What to do / Must NOT do: Per the findings: correct the backup copy logic, re-check the armed-timer rule on save, propagate store errors, one typed-name confirmation, meaningful finish-recovery budget tests (dedupe the two suites), shrink unused public/non_exhaustive surface, one step-count constant, delete dead template calls and the unused Batch path. Findings closed by this todo (fix each one; where a critic correction is given it overrides the reviewer's text):
  - `W2-06` (low; critic confirmed) The config page's backup "copy" logic is built on a false premise. Evidence: `config_page/store.rs:122-124`, `kcc-app/src/backups/store.rs:86-108`, `store.rs:264-285`. Fix: Call `store.create` directly. Delete `STORING`, the copy loop and `Taken`, and drop the `--N` handling.
  - `W2-09` (low; critic adjusted) Save does not re-check the "not while the timer is armed" rule the page shows. Evidence: `partials/config_status.html:19`, `config_page/safety.rs:66-80`. Fix: Refuse Save while `fail_safe.active` is true, audited as `armed`, mirroring `while_armed`. Critic correction (binding): Reachable only when the timer runs with no lease held; the router-side consequence is unverified. Fix as proposed.
  - `W2-11` (low; critic adjusted) Store errors are swallowed or downgraded. Evidence: `config_page/create.rs:98-104`, `restore.rs:105-108`, `wizard/store.rs:480-484`, `events.rs:137-140`. Fix: Map the list error to `stopped(.., stored(locale, error))`. Log the failed loads with `tracing::warn!`. Critic correction (binding): The config-page part is a wrong user answer and a missing audit entry; it is logged. The wizard part is a truly silent swallow.
  - `W2-12` (low; critic adjusted) The typed-router-name confirmation is implemented three times. Evidence: `confirm/stakes.rs:88-99`, `confirm/prompt.rs:74-89`, `config_page/confirm.rs:240-253`, `wizard/finish.rs:164-183`. Fix: Have the wizard and config page use `Guard::Destructive.check` plus a single dialog builder. Use `RAW_RESTORE.action` instead of the literal. Critic correction (binding): There are two implementations (the kit and the wizard), not three. Fix: have the wizard use `Guard::Destructive.check`, and use `RAW_RESTORE.action` in `restore.rs:125`.
  - `W2-14` (low; critic confirmed) The finish-recovery "budget" tests barely assert anything, and the two suites are copies. Evidence: `tests/wizard_finish_recovery.rs:265-274`, `tests/preset_finish_recovery.rs:244-253`. Fix: Assert the expected status and the audit outcome. Move the shared scenarios into one generic helper parameterized by the wizard.
  - `W2-16` (info; critic adjusted, low->info) Public or `non_exhaustive` API surface that nothing outside the crate uses. Evidence: `table.rs:15,17`, `lib.rs:118`, `submit/rejected.rs:7`, `submit/unplanned.rs:10`. Fix: Make them `pub(crate)` and drop the boilerplate attributes. Critic correction (binding): The fix is narrowing visibility to `pub(crate)`; the attributes on public items are profile-mandated. Concrete cost is negligible.
  - `W2-17` (info; critic adjusted, low->info) The step count is a magic number in two places. Evidence: `wizard/flow.rs:90`, `wizard/screen.rs:399`, `flow.rs:99-105`. Fix: Use `F::Step::ALL.len()` for the total. Critic correction (binding): Use `ALL.len()` for the total; this is a cosmetic duplication only.
  - `W2-18` (low; critic confirmed) Dead template calls and an unused Batch path in the kit. Evidence: `partials/system_general_facts.html:9`, `system_general/fields.rs:57-64`, `page_kit/action.rs:228`, `advanced/deliver.rs`. Fix: Drop the two template calls. Remove the Batch arm from `Action::run`, or make it report partial results.

- [ ] 58. Admin and auth core leftovers; first-party JS cleanup
  What to do / Must NOT do: Per the findings: dedupe admin/auth helpers and make bare 500s match the docs; one permission list that rejects unknown permissions; explicit arms instead of wildcards for future variants (W1-12: `_` in `plan.rs` returns an error, and the audit label for `_` is `unknown`); a new API key is shown even if the refresh fails (render the key first, then refresh); the CSRF test sets one rejecting condition per case; app.js drops review labels and its three copies of the swap override. Findings closed by this todo (fix each one; where a critic correction is given it overrides the reviewer's text):
  - `W1-09` (low; critic confirmed) Copy-pasted helpers in the admin and auth modules, and the bare 500s don't match the docs. Evidence: `auth.rs:567-570`, `admin_users.rs:781-787`, `admin_roles.rs:807-813`, `admin_keys.rs:446-452`. Fix: Move `manages` and `RoleOption` into `admin_shared`. Delete the 4 `internal`s in favour of `errors::internal` with `fragment_shell`. Keep one cookie parser. Build `role_label` from `built_in_roles()`.
  - `W1-11` (info; critic adjusted, low->info) The permission list is hand-maintained in 3 places and silently drops unknown permissions. Evidence: `crates/kcc-web/src/admin_roles.rs:69-108`, `errors/refusal.rs:63`. Fix: Derive the label and field name from an exhaustive `match permission { ... }`, iterate over `Permission::ALL`, and parse the form as `Vec<(String, String)>`. Critic correction (binding): Use a unit test asserting that the `PERMISSIONS` column equals `Permission::ALL`, and parse the form generically. An exhaustive match will not give compile-time protection here.
  - `W1-12` (info; critic adjusted, low->info) Wildcard arms map unknown future variants to a specific behaviour. Evidence: `router_form/plan.rs:189-192`, `router_access.rs:67-70`, `router_form.rs:103-106`. Fix: For these kcc-owned `#[non_exhaustive]` enums, handle `_` as an explicit error or a neutral value ("unknown"), not as the most privileged known variant. Critic correction (binding): Optionally route `_` to a neutral or error value in `plan.rs` and to an "unknown" audit label. Nothing goes wrong today.
  - `W1-16` (low; critic confirmed) A new API key is lost if the page refresh or render fails after creation. Evidence: `crates/kcc-web/src/admin_keys.rs:220-249`. Fix: Render the plaintext dialog even when the region refresh fails (the refresh is optional), or revoke the key on that error path.
  - `W1-18` (low; critic adjusted) The cross-site CSRF test sets two rejecting conditions at once. Evidence: `crates/kcc-web/tests/auth.rs:251-262`. Fix: Split the test into 2 cases: a bad Origin with no `sec-fetch-site`, and a good Origin with `cross-site`. Add one positive case (valid token and Origin gives 2xx or 3xx). Critic correction (binding): Add 2 cases: a bad or missing Origin with no `sec-fetch-site`, and a good Origin with `cross-site`. No new positive case is needed. ## Summary - CONFIRMED: 8 (W1-01, 02, 04, 09, 10, 13, 16, 17) - ADJUSTED: 10 (W1-03 to low, 05 still medium, 06 still medium, 07 to low, 08 to low, 11 to info, 12 to info, 14 still low, 15 to info, 18 still low) - REFUTED: 0 - DUPLICATE: 0 - After review: 0 critical, 0 high, 5 medium, 10 low, 3 info. **NEW: the CLI `router edit` has the same seal-before-update flaw as W1-05.** It is outside lane W1. In `crates/kcc/src/cli/router_secrets.rs:120-122`, `point(...)` and then `seal(store, &id, &secrets)?` run before `services.registry().update(&role, ...
  - `SEC1-13` (low; critic confirmed) `app.js` carries review-process labels and three copies of the same swap override. Evidence: `crates/kcc-web/assets/js/app.js:322,364,388,412,427,443`. Fix: Remove the labels. Merge into one `beforeSwap` handler with a status table.
  - `W1-10` (low; critic confirmed) Review-process labels shipped in production JavaScript. Evidence: `crates/kcc-web/assets/js/app.js:322,364,388,412,427,443`. Fix: Drop the `todo NN:` prefixes and keep the behavioural sentence.

Evidence: `migrate_wizard/apply.rs:46-100`, `presets_page/apply.rs:49-112`, `launch.rs`, `target_confirm.rs:42-86`. Fix: Move `apply` + `launch` + `settle` into `crate::wizard` behind a `Flow` hook for "record end". Move `attachment`, `fact`, the password check and `filters` into wizard or page_kit helpers, and the shared helpers out of `migrate_wizard`. Critic correction (binding): Use the line references above. This is maintainability, not a medium bug.
  - `W5-08` (low; critic confirmed) Cross-crate reason keys and display text are matched as strings, and unknown keys fall into wildcard arms. Evidence: `migrate_wizard/reasons.rs:136-161`, `warnings.rs:26,219-229`, `needs.rs:37`, `presets_page/fates.rs:55-58,117-119`. Fix: Have kcc-migrate expose typed reason or input enums (`NeededInput::Install(name)`, `PasswordUser(name)`) and match them exhaustively.
  - `W5-10` (low; critic adjusted) Errors are swallowed or recorded as the wrong outcome. Evidence: `presets_page/reveal.rs:99-105`, `migrate_wizard/preview.rs:393`, `inputs.rs:169`, `apply_request.rs:71`. Fix: Map the I/O and crypto errors to `errors::internal` and record an audit outcome of `failed`. Critic correction (binding): Drop the `compose.rs` bullet and fix the preview line to `migrate_wizard/preview.rs:122`.
  - `W5-14` (info; critic adjusted, low->info) Maps go through a JSON round-trip and silently become empty if it fails. Evidence: `migrate_wizard/maps.rs:265-274`, `kcc-migrate/src/maps.rs:22-25`. Fix: `let mut map = M::default(); map.entries = pairs.map(MapEntry::new)...` (or a small trait for the three map types).
  - `W5-15` (info; critic adjusted, low->info) Two stacked firmware-update caches with different retry rules, and a doc comment that describes the wrong one. Evidence: `fleet.rs:220-222`, `fleet_updates.rs:391-418`. Fix: Keep one cache (the one in `ComponentsUpdateCheck`) and make `UpdateCache` a thin pass-through, or drop it. Critic correction (binding): Cite `fleet_updates.rs:117-144`. The impact is a misleading comment plus negligible spawns.
  - `W5-17` (low; critic confirmed) Test gaps: the cross-user check covers only GET, and tests assert on markup fragments. Evidence: `tests/migration_wizard/plan_steps.rs:197-200`, `tests/migration_wizard/records.rs:106-111`, `presets_pages/secrets.rs:140`, `tests/dashboard.rs`. Fix: Add one table-driven stranger test over every run route of both wizards. Assert on parsed elements or ids instead of markup. Move the router-form tests out of `dashboard.rs`.

- [ ] 56. Pre-rendered HTML is typed, not `String` + `|safe`
  What to do / Must NOT do: Replace every HTML `String` rendered with `|safe` by a private `Rendered` newtype whose only constructor is `rendered(&impl Template)` (SEC1-10, W2-15, W3-17), and type the slots `Option<Rendered>`, so plain strings can never reach `|safe`; template lint (`cargo xtask assets-check`) rejects `|safe` on anything but that type. Golden HTML must not change: capture with the previous plan's script approach before and diff after. Findings closed by this todo (fix each one; where a critic correction is given it overrides the reviewer's text):
  - `SEC1-10` (low; critic adjusted, medium->low) HTML is passed around as plain `String` and rendered with `|safe`, against "askama auto-escaping only, no bypass". Evidence: `crates/kcc-web/templates/partials/kit_result.html:4,10,13`, `crates/kcc-web/src/page_kit/respond.rs:122-126`, `routing_page/reply.rs:64`. Fix: Add a `Markup(String)` newtype whose only constructor is `rendered(&impl Template)`. Make these fields `Option<Markup>` so `|safe` applies only to that type. Critic correction (binding): The fix stands: a `Markup` newtype that only `rendered()` can construct. Severity is low because no producer of unescaped text exists today.
  - `W2-15` (low; critic confirmed) Pre-rendered HTML passes through `|safe` as plain `String`. Evidence: `partials/kit_result.html:4,10,13`, `respond.rs:115-127`. Fix: Add a `Rendered(String)` newtype whose only constructor is `request::rendered`, and use it in these signatures.
  - `W3-17` (info; critic confirmed) `|safe` on composed regions. Evidence: `pages/net_interfaces.html:20`, `net_internet.html:19`, `clients.html:16`, `dhcp.html:26`. Fix: Type the slots as `Option<Rendered>` (a newtype only `rendered()` can build), so plain strings can't reach `|safe`.
  Must NOT: change unrelated behaviour, add a crate that is not already in `[workspace.dependencies]` or `Cargo.lock`, weaken a security control, change the audit entry format or hash rule, or touch files owned by another open todo of the same wave beyond what this todo needs; keep routes, MCP tool names/schemas and CLI flags except where a finding above changes them on purpose (name each such change in the commit body).

- [ ] 61. CLI behaviour fixes
  What to do / Must NOT do: Per the findings: `backup diff` compares ordered unredacted lines (redact the printed output) and its help says what it does; one confirmation gate per action; rollback results report saved state correctly; VPN secret writes take their gate and `unsaved` from the plan; `--json` covers argument errors; fix the `-` router in confirmation messages. Findings closed by this todo (fix each one; where a critic correction is given it overrides the reviewer's text):
  - `F-07` (medium; critic adjusted) `backup diff` compares sets of redacted lines, so it misses real changes, and its help text says "Count". Evidence: `crates/kcc/src/cli/persist.rs:176-186`, `args_fleet.rs:139`, `kcc-web/src/config_page/text.rs:36-43`. Fix: Diff the full-text digests in order (as the Web does), print `shown[i]`, and fix the help text. Critic correction (binding): Drop the claim about `up`/`exit`/`shutdown` in other blocks. The defect is invisible secret changes, plus set semantics for reordering and exact duplicates.
  - `F-12` (low; critic adjusted, medium->low) Inconsistent confirmation gates for the same action. Evidence: `migrate_apply.rs:49`, `preset/run.rs:75`, `migrate.rs:170-171`, `preset/finish.rs:91`. Fix: When `finish == Rollback`, also run `confirm(Risk::Destructive, ...)`. Give `preset delete` a confirmation.
  - `F-15` (low; critic confirmed) Rollback results report `"unsaved": true`. Evidence: `persist.rs:130`, `migrate.rs:185`, `change.rs:286-288`. Fix: Use `applied(false)` for rollback, or take the flag from the closing result.
  - `F-16` (info; critic adjusted, low->info) VPN secret writes use a fixed gate and a hard-coded `unsaved`. Evidence: `dom_vpn.rs:101-105`, `dom_vpn_server.rs:54-58`. Fix: Have `client_secrets` and `server_secrets` return a `ChangeOp` (risk from the planner) and use `Gate::of` plus `result.unsaved`.
  - `F-27` (low; critic confirmed) `--json` contract is broken for argument errors. Evidence: `main.rs:20-25`. Fix: When `--json` is present in argv, wrap clap errors in the JSON envelope.
  - `F-28` (info; critic adjusted, low->info) Root cause of the `-` router in confirmation messages (KNOWN-BACKLOG). Evidence: `secret.rs:118-126`, `dom_jobs.rs:242-243`, `principal.rs:86`, `preset/manage.rs:33`. Fix: Pass the router into `ask`.

- [ ] 63. `kcc mcp stdio` handles signals like `kcc serve`
  What to do / Must NOT do: On SIGTERM/SIGINT, stop accepting input, wait for open brackets (BRACKET_GRACE), drain jobs and flush the audit log as `kcc serve` does. Test with a real child process and a held bracket (pattern: crates/kcc/tests/serve_bracket/). Findings closed by this todo (fix each one; where a critic correction is given it overrides the reviewer's text):
  - `F-05` (medium; critic confirmed) `kcc mcp stdio` has no signal handling, so a signal skips the bracket wait, job drain and audit flush. Evidence: `crates/kcc/src/server.rs:234-235`, `rmcp-server-kit-3.21.1/src/transport.rs:5916-5937`. Fix: Wrap `serve_stdio` in a `select!` with `tokio::signal::ctrl_c()` and a SIGTERM stream, then fall through to the existing wait, drain and flush.

  What to do / Must NOT do: Per the findings. B-22: collapse the two-group env-override macro to one row list and normalize suffixes once after `apply_rows`, KEEPING today's error precedence (the critic's point) - add a test where a bad suffix and a later bad variable are both set and the same error is reported first as before.   - `B-13` (low; critic adjusted) Router-profile and router-id validation is duplicated and has drifted. Evidence: `settings.rs:958-1006`, `registry.rs:108-134`, `registry/auth.rs:16-31`, `settings.rs:645`. Fix: Have `RouterProfile::validate` call one shared profile validator, use `is_valid_router_id` everywhere, and fix the doc. Critic correction (binding): Drop the auth/token_file claim. Keep the URL-shape gap, the doc mismatch and the duplicated id rule.
  - `B-16` (info; critic adjusted, low->info) `glob_matches` claims to be kit-compatible but is not. Evidence: `registry.rs:962-966`, `rmcp-server-kit-3.21.1/src/rbac.rs:1118-1130`. Fix: Match the kit (case-insensitive, empty means none, with the CLI passing `["*"]`), or fix the comment. Critic correction (binding): Fix the comment. The behavioural impact needs state-file tampering.
  - `B-22` (info; critic adjusted, low->info) The two-group env-override macro adds structure nothing needs. Evidence: `settings/env_overrides.rs:30-55,137-139`. Fix: Collapse to one row list and normalize once after `apply_rows`.
  - `B-11` (low; critic confirmed) Module-wide `#![expect]` blankets with "the plan mandates" reasons hide wildcard arms. Evidence: `crates/kcc-app/src/workflow.rs:2-13`, `registry.rs:2-16`, `jobs.rs:2-9`, `poller.rs:2-9`. Fix: Move each `expect` onto the item that needs it, with a code-level reason, and drop `wildcard_enum_match_arm` from the module level.
  - `B-24` (low; critic confirmed) A route transition on a `kcc.toml` router keeps the new URL only in memory but rewrites the operator's password file. Evidence: `registry.rs:358-365`, `registry/stored.rs:54-65`. Fix: Refuse URL transitions for config-defined routers, or persist neither change and report the required `kcc.toml` edit.

- [ ] 66. Cancel-safety annotations everywhere and a gate that checks private async fns
  What to do / Must NOT do: Extend `cargo xtask cancel-safety` to private and `pub(crate)` async fns (closing the BACKLOG item at docs/BACKLOG.md:77) and annotate every async fn it finds (about 425: kcc-web ~297, kcc-mcp ~71, kcc-app ~20). One rule for axum/MCP handlers: read-only handlers are `// cancel-safe:` (a client disconnect drops the future, nothing changes); handlers that send a router change or write state are `// NOT cancel-safe:` with the reason. A-18: one rule for logins: a cancelled login may leave an orphaned router session, so login fns are `// NOT cancel-safe:` with that reason, and their callers follow. This todo lands as ONE commit (D-DELIVERY); todos after it (67-73, 75) must annotate the async fns they add. Run `cargo test --manifest-path xtask/Cargo.toml --all-features` and `cargo clippy --manifest-path xtask/Cargo.toml --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings`. The gate must fail on a missing annotation (test with a scratch fn, shown then reverted). This todo owns every cancel-safety finding (B-18, G-20, E-11, W5-09, A-18). Findings closed by this todo (fix each one; where a critic correction is given it overrides the reviewer's text):
  - `B-18` (info; critic adjusted, low->info) More private async functions lack cancel-safety annotations than BACKLOG lists (KNOWN-BACKLOG). Evidence: `poller.rs:306`, `poller/extended.rs:72,125,153`, `registry.rs:767`, `jobs.rs:511,546,571`. Fix: Add the annotations and update the BACKLOG list. Critic correction (binding): Treat it as a correction to the BACKLOG list, not a new finding.
  - `G-20` (low; critic adjusted) Wrong or missing cancel-safety annotations. Evidence: `apply/finish.rs:134`, `apply/verify.rs:95`, `apply/preflight.rs:132`, `logs/service.rs:241`. Fix: correct the `recover` comment to NOT cancel-safe and add the missing annotations. Critic correction (binding): The new part is the wrong `recover` comment. The missing annotations are instances of BACKLOG:77.
  - `E-11` (low; critic adjusted, medium->low) About 70 async fns have no cancel-safety annotation, including mutating handlers. Evidence: `domains/write/wifi.rs:114`, `clients.rs:210,224`, `interfaces.rs:105,189`, `services.rs:185`. Fix: Add `// NOT cancel-safe:` / `// cancel-safe:` comments and extend the xtask to `pub(crate)` and private items. Critic correction (binding): These are new instances of a known BACKLOG class. Add them to that item, or annotate them.
  - `W5-09` (low; critic confirmed) About 18 handlers and helpers have no cancel-safety comment. Evidence: `migrate_wizard/apply.rs:46`, `downloads.rs:39`, `inputs_submit.rs:57`, `landing.rs:104,174`. Fix: Add the comments, and extend the xtask check to `pub(crate)` and `pub(super)`.
  - `A-18` (low; critic confirmed) Cancel-safety comments contradict each other. Evidence: `engine.rs:238-240`, `session.rs:345-346`, `session.rs:449-450`, `engine.rs:257,268`. Fix: Use one rule: a cancelled login that leaves only a remote session is or is not cancel-safe, chosen once. Correct the callers to match the callees.

- [ ] 67. Secret-leak tests that can fail; a fake router that does not mask kcc's work
  What to do / Must NOT do: Per the findings: test secrets without `"`/`\` (and one that has them, asserted in its escaped form too); the fake's request log keeps every line of a batch, attributed per request; the fake stores what kcc sends so 'kcc hashes / never stores plaintext' tests check kcc. Each changed test must be shown failing against a deliberate leak (scratch change, then reverted) in the evidence. Findings closed by this todo (fix each one; where a critic correction is given it overrides the reviewer's text):
  - `T-01` (medium; critic confirmed) Secret-leak tests cannot fail for JSON, `Debug` or `?`-formatted traces: each secret contains `"` (and `\`). Evidence: `crates/kcc-app/tests/secrets_write.rs:72`, `crates/kcc-domain/tests/users.rs:77`, `crates/kcc-domain/tests/wifi.rs:134,140-161`, `wan.rs:119,125-150`. Fix: Check each sink against every encoding the secret can take there: `serde_json::to_string(secret)` without its outer quotes, `secret.escape_debug()`, and the CLI-quoted form. Alternatively, add a second secret with no characters that need escaping and run the checks on both. Critic correction (binding): Scope note: "only catch leaks printed raw" is overstated. `kcc/tests/cli_domain_writes/secrets.rs` (`PASSWORD = "s3cret-Vpn-Passw0rd"`, WireGuard keys) and `kcc-migrate/tests/support/apply_secrets.rs` use quote-free secrets against the audit log. The `kcc-app` scrub test and the four domain suites listed are vacuous.
  - `T-03` (low; critic adjusted, medium->low) Fake router request log keeps only the last parse line of a batch and can misattribute lines under concurrency. Evidence: `crates/kcc-testkit/src/server.rs:439-453`, `server.rs:974-976`, `server.rs:416-436`, `kcc-app/src/workflow.rs:1284-1292`. Fix: Record parse lines per request, not at `last_mut()`. Carry a record index in a request extension set by the middleware, and store `Vec<Option<String>>` of lines per record. Document batch behaviour in BEHAVIOUR.md. Critic correction (binding): This is a fidelity bug in the fake that could mask future absence assertions. No current test is demonstrated to be wrong.
  - `T-04` (low; critic adjusted, medium->low) The fake hashes or encodes plaintext secrets itself, so "kcc hashes / never stores plaintext" tests check the fake; the conversion is undocumented. Evidence: `crates/kcc-testkit/src/cli_engine/secrets.rs:84-104`, `kcc-domain/src/router_users.rs:217`, `users.rs`, `wifi.rs`. Fix: Mark the plain→md5 and plaintext→NS3 conversions **assumed** in BEHAVIOUR.md. In the tests, assert on what kcc sent (the parse line before the fake's conversion), not on the stored text. Add `"ike key"` to the request-log secret markers. Critic correction (binding): Drop "tests check the fake, not kcc" as a defect. Keep these items: the undocumented conversions, the md5-versus-HA1 inconsistency, and the missing `ike key` marker.
  Must NOT: change unrelated behaviour, add a crate that is not already in `[workspace.dependencies]` or `Cargo.lock`, weaken a security control, change the audit entry format or hash rule, or touch files owned by another open todo of the same wave beyond what this todo needs; keep routes, MCP tool names/schemas and CLI flags except where a finding above changes them on purpose (name each such change in the commit body).

- [ ] 68. Live suites reach real routers only through the safety contract
  What to do / Must NOT do: Per the findings: live_presets/live_mcp can reach a real router (no `allow_loopback` dependence) under the same serial allowlist and `test_device` gates; the live migration source read checks the serial allowlist; live_mcp_smoke prints `live-skip:` (install a tracing subscriber: add `tracing-subscriber = { workspace = true, features = ["fmt", "std"] }` to kcc-mcp's dev-dependencies, the form kcc-app and kcc-migrate use, because the workspace entry disables default features); read-only suites require a `test_device = true` profile; the probe asserts instead of recording. Do not set KCC_LIVE_* - verify with the hermetic refusal tests. Findings closed by this todo (fix each one; where a critic correction is given it overrides the reviewer's text):
  - `T-02` (medium; critic confirmed) `live_presets` and `live_mcp` cannot reach a real router; their twins hide it with `allow_loopback`. Evidence: `crates/kcc/tests/live_presets.rs:186-190`, `crates/kcc-mcp/tests/live_mcp/session.rs:218-220`, `crates/kcc-app/src/registry.rs:387-395`, `kcc-rci/src/outbound.rs:254-259`. Fix: Copy `outbound` from the admitted `KCC_LIVE_TARGET_CONFIG` into the hermetic `kcc.toml` and into the MCP registry, as `live_domains/campaign.rs:157-161` and `live_migration/rehearsal.rs:211-215` already do. In the twins, set `allowed_networks` instead of relying on loopback alone.
  - `T-05` (low; critic adjusted, medium->low) Live migration reads a live source router without the serial allowlist. Evidence: `crates/kcc/tests/live_migration/rehearsal.rs:260-273`, `live_migration.rs:119-127`. Fix: Call `connect` plus `verify_serial(&client, &live.allowed)` on the source before `registry.create`, and return a `Refusal::SerialNotAllowed` for it. Critic correction (binding): This is a contract violation on a read-only path the operator opts into explicitly, so low rather than medium. The fix is unchanged.
  - `T-06` (low; critic confirmed) `live_mcp_smoke` skips silently: no tracing subscriber, so `live-skip:` is never printed. Evidence: `crates/kcc-mcp/tests/live_mcp.rs:79-89`, `live_mcp/session.rs`, `live_smoke.rs:78`. Fix: Add `drop(tracing_subscriber::fmt().with_writer(io::stderr).try_init());` as the first line, and warn when writes are requested without a config.
  - `T-08` (low; critic confirmed) Read-only live suites contact routers without a `test_device = true` profile. Evidence: `crates/kcc/tests/live_smoke.rs:87-93`, `live_domains/gate.rs:59-62`, `kcc-mcp/tests/live_mcp/session.rs:85-89`. Fix: Require `KCC_LIVE_TARGET_CONFIG` with `test_device = true` for every live suite, and keep the serial check as the second gate. If read-only runs on non-test devices are intended, record that as an owner decision in the project overlay.
  - `T-09` (low; critic adjusted) Live migration probe writes after the safety net and records results instead of asserting them. Evidence: `crates/kcc/tests/live_migration/rehearsal.rs:126-134`, `live_migration/probe.rs:74-94`, `live_domains/matrix.rs:99-115`. Fix: Run the probe inside an armed fail-safe timer, or before the rollback, and remove the account on every path. Assert that `target_realm_hash_accepted == Some(true)` before trusting the source-realm result. Critic correction (binding): Drop the `live_campaign_matrix` sub-claim and keep the probe ordering, cleanup and missing control assertion.
  - `F-31` (info; critic confirmed) `live_smoke` checks only the serial allowlist, not `test_device`. Evidence: `tests/live_smoke.rs:61-92`, `live_domains/gate.rs:96`, `live_migration/guard.rs:74`. Fix: Require the `KCC_LIVE_TARGET_CONFIG` profile check here too, or document the exemption in the overlay.
  Must NOT: change unrelated behaviour, add a crate that is not already in `[workspace.dependencies]` or `Cargo.lock`, weaken a security control, change the audit entry format or hash rule, or touch files owned by another open todo of the same wave beyond what this todo needs; keep routes, MCP tool names/schemas and CLI flags except where a finding above changes them on purpose (name each such change in the commit body).

- [ ] 69. Test hygiene across the workspace
  What to do / Must NOT do: Per the findings: no bind-0-then-drop port reservations for child processes: the test harness retries the spawn on `EADDRINUSE` a bounded number of times (no `listen_port = 0` product feature, settings require >= 1024); one bracket fate matrix with thin per-surface wiring; T-11: replace the source grep with a `clippy::disallowed_methods` entry for process spawning (keep only that half; a refused-uid run test needs root); no Debug-string equality; no vacuous assertions or prose pins; deterministic time windows; remove review-process labels from tests and code (A-22, F-29); every deleted test listed with its replacement in the evidence ledger. Findings closed by this todo (fix each one; where a critic correction is given it overrides the reviewer's text):
  - `T-07` (low; critic confirmed) Ports reserved by bind-0-then-drop are reused by child processes. Evidence: `crates/kcc/tests/serve_bracket/process.rs:91`, `metrics_serve.rs:99-102`, `metrics_serve/runtime.rs:62`, `serve.rs:242-246`. Fix: Have `kcc serve` accept `listen_port = 0` and log the bound address, then parse it from the "listening" line. Alternatively, pass an inherited, already-bound socket.
  - `T-10` (info; critic adjusted, low->info) Bracket fate matrices are copied across the Web, MCP and domain tests. Evidence: `kcc-web/tests/explorer_pages/bracket.rs:111-142`, `kcc-mcp/tests/support/bracket_failures.rs:47-92`, `bracket_batch.rs:19-31`, `bracket_batch.rs:25-37`. Fix: Keep the full fate matrix in `kcc-domain`. Have each surface test one row per fate to its code and message, from a shared table in `kcc-testkit` or test support. Critic correction (binding): Move the arm/disarm lines and fault constructors into `kcc-testkit`. The per-surface rows are justified.
  - `T-11` (low; critic adjusted) Source-grep tests stand in for behaviour tests. Evidence: `crates/kcc/tests/source_guards.rs:37-40`. Fix: Test the privilege floor through the binary (run as a uid that should be refused, check exit code 77). Express the subprocess ban as a `clippy::disallowed_methods`/`disallowed_types` entry for `std::process::Command`. Critic correction (binding): Bypass example: `use std::process::{Command as Run}`. Exit-code coverage of the privilege floor exists outside this test.
  - `T-12` (info; critic confirmed) `Debug` strings compared as an equality proxy. Evidence: `crates/kcc-web/src/page_kit/request.rs:173-176`. Fix: Derive `PartialEq` for `TableQuery` in tests, or compare its fields.
  - `F-29` (low; critic adjusted) Test quality: a vacuous assertion, prose pins and a review label. Evidence: `tests/cli_domain_writes/admin.rs:15`, `tests/cli/outbound.rs:62`, `permission_gates/apply.rs:228`, `cli/health.rs:103`. Fix: Parse the JSON and compare fields, assert localized `tr(...)` strings, and drop the label. Critic correction (binding): Drop `outbound.rs:62` and `serve.rs:440` from the prose-pin list.
  - `F-30` (low; critic confirmed) Test quality: port race, copied helpers, time windows, source greps. Evidence: `serve.rs:242-247`, `serve_origin.rs:211`, `metrics_serve.rs:100`, `cli/health.rs:31`. Fix: Pass `listen_port = 0` and read the bound port from the log, put shared helpers in a `tests/support` module, and wait on the drain log line instead of the window.
  - `B-23` (low; critic confirmed) Several tests cannot fail, describe the wrong failure, or wait in real time. Evidence: `jobs.rs:822-834`, `tests/jobs_recovery.rs:242`, `tests/audit.rs:317-323`, `tests/workflow.rs:147-149`. Fix: Cancel after the reservation (hold `write_jobs` instead), force a collision with `for_test` allocators, rename the misleading test, wait on the watch signal, and assert on a structured field.
  - `C-25` (low; critic confirmed) Tests that cannot fail or miss the behavior they name. Evidence: `tests/system.rs:117-131`, `tests/components.rs:65-73`, `verification_coverage.rs:96-99`, `administration.rs:142-145`. Fix: Call `factory_reset` against the fixture, assert the job's progress payload, and fail on registry writes the catalog does not have.
  - `D-23` (low; critic adjusted) Tests that duplicate, cannot fail, or cost a lot for little. Evidence: `tests/firewall.rs:153-174`, `tests/firewall_binding.rs:197-218`, `tests/vpn.rs:173-175`, `vpn_servers.rs:48-96`. Fix: Delete the duplicate and the vacuous assertions, and replace the address cross-product with the property and clear cases. Critic correction (binding): Drop the "vacuous assertion" sub-claim and fix the duplicate's line numbers. The coverage gap for D-01 stands. ## Summary - CONFIRMED: 11 (D-03, D-04, D-05, D-06, D-08, D-09, D-11, D-12, D-17, D-20, D-21) - ADJUSTED: 12 (D-01, D-02, D-07, D-10, D-13, D-14, D-15, D-16, D-18, D-19, D-22, D-23) - REFUTED: 0 - DUPLICATE: 0 NEW issues, to be validated separately: - **NEW-1 (D-01 sibling, high):** the Web schedule or description dialog, saved with nothing to clear (the default empty field, or "no schedule" on a rule without a schedule), sends `no access-list L rule N disable`. `Expect::Property` (`expect.rs:85-100`) still holds, so a disabled firewall rule is silently re-enabled and audited as ...
  - `A-21` (low; critic adjusted) Tests that cannot fail, duplicated scenarios, and source-grep tests. Evidence: `crates/kcc-config/tests/config_text.rs:25-29`, `ns3.rs`, `crates/kcc-rci/tests/config_accessor.rs:17-22,63-79`, `crates/kcc-i18n/src/locale.rs:156-159`. Fix: Delete the tautology, the duplicate and the const pin. Replace the grep with a type-level guard (already present per its own header). Critic correction (binding): Only one test cannot fail (the tautology). Keep the cookie-name pin.
  - `E-23` (low; critic adjusted) Test quality: tautologies, tests that pin known-broken behavior, and review labels. Evidence: `crates/kcc-mcp/src/tests/random.rs:37-61`, `src/tests/gates.rs:196`, `tests/support/curated_bracket.rs:209-210`, `tests/support/interface_refusals.rs:72`. Fix: Assert specific codes per input class, fix the fake rather than pinning it, and drop task labels from test code. Critic correction (binding): Keep the `random.rs`, `gates.rs:196` and `interface_refusals` items. Drop the evidence-path, Bridge0 and caps items.
  - `G-25` (low; critic confirmed) Test quality: real bugs above have no tests, and some test code is noise. Evidence: `tests/logs.rs:58-88`, `tests/rules.rs:448-455`, `tests/finish_recovery.rs:60,81,119,160`, `finish_recovery.rs:72,108,151,185`. Fix: add the four missing scenarios, use `kcc_testkit::DEFAULT_PASSWORD`, and remove the cancel-safety comments from tests.
  - `A-22` (info; critic confirmed) Review-process labels left in code and tests. Evidence: `crates/kcc-rci/tests/auth_survivors.rs:1-2`, `tests/auth_parser.rs:18,55`, `crates/kcc-catalog/src/request.rs:168-170`. Fix: Rewrite each as a statement of the behaviour it pins.

- [ ] 70. CI workflows: branches, gates, release checks, token handling, image tags

`persist-credentials: false` on checkouts (X-08); prerelease tags never move `:latest`, the version label comes from the tag (X-09); `scripts/ci-local.sh` matches ci.yml (X-12); X-13: the profile keeps only x86_64 and aarch64 (change the five hard-coded architectures in `xtask/src/seccomp_gen.rs:133-138`, regenerate the profile by the documented trace procedure (`deploy/podman/README.md:127-133`), made stable against run-to-run variation: `podman build --format docker -t kcc:test .`; copy `/kcc` out of the image into `${CARGO_TARGET_DIR:-target}/kcc-musl`; trace twice, `cargo xtask seccomp trace --binary <that file>` (it drives `deploy/podman/e2e.sh --native` under strace into `<target>/seccomp-trace`) and `cargo xtask seccomp trace --binary <that file> --out <target>/seccomp-trace-2`; copy both sets of `trace*` files into `<target>/seccomp-trace-union`, adding the suffix `.1` or `.2` so names cannot clash (`gen` reads every file whose name starts with `trace`); run `cargo xtask seccomp gen --check --traces <target>/seccomp-trace-union` to list the added and removed syscalls (it exits non-zero when the profile differs, so do not chain it with `&&` or run it under `set -e` before the next command), then `cargo xtask seccomp gen --traces <target>/seccomp-trace-union`; if `gen` reports unmapped syscalls, assign each a group in `xtask/src/seccomp_groups.rs` (an xtask change, named in the commit body, covered by the xtask test and clippy runs); commit the profile and name every added and removed syscall in the commit body (never edit the profile by hand); update deploy/podman/README.md with the two-trace procedure; prove the new profile with `bash deploy/podman/e2e.sh --container --image kcc:test` and remove the image with `podman rmi kcc:test`), and the drift check blocks only when a traced syscall is missing from the profile (not for extras, which vary between traces): add a `--check-missing` mode to `cargo xtask seccomp gen` that fails only on traced syscalls absent from the committed profile, with an xtask test, and make the drift step in `.github/workflows/container.yml:86-91` run it without `continue-on-error`. Fix: Use `branches: [master]` in `ci.yml` and `container.yml`, or rename the default branch and update CONTRIBUTING.md. Then make the CI jobs required status checks. - [ ] 75. Every dependency and gate tool at the latest version past the cooldown (D-LATEST; overlay deviation 3)
  What to do / Must NOT do: Owner request 2026-11-15 (D-LATEST): every crate at the latest possible version, which the core Version Policy (`docs/rust-guidelines/RUST_GUIDELINES.md` Section 12 "Version Policy" and Section 10 "publish-age cooldown") defines as the latest version that has passed the dependency cooldown (`.cargo/config.toml`: `global-min-publish-age = "3 days"`, `incompatible-publish-age = "deny"`); this closes project-overlay deviation 3. Run it at execution time, after every other code todo (versions move). Steps: (1) Run `cargo update --dry-run --verbose` (root) and `cargo update --dry-run --verbose --manifest-path xtask/Cargo.toml` (it uses the same `.cargo/config.toml`). A line `Unchanged <crate> vX (available: vY)` WITHOUT `published ... ago`, for a crate that is a direct dependency and where vY is semver-incompatible with the current requirement, is a newer incompatible release: raise that requirement to vY in `[workspace.dependencies]`, the member manifest or `xtask/Cargo.toml`, keeping `default-features` and the feature list and checking that every enabled feature still exists in vY (on 2026-11-15 the only one was `sha3` 0.11.0 -> 0.12.0, which keeps `zeroize`, needs Rust 1.85 and adds the new MIT/Apache transitive crate `sponge-cursor` 0.1; kcc-rci's `getrandom` 0.2 -> 0.4 is todo 71's). A line WITH `published ... ago` is held by the cooldown and is NOT taken; a compatible `available` version that a plain update does not take is held by a dependent's requirement (for example `matchit` by `axum`'s `=0.8.4`). (2) Run a plain `cargo update` in both workspaces, never with `CARGO_RESOLVER_INCOMPATIBLE_PUBLISH_AGE=allow` (that is only the RUSTSEC emergency path), and review both `Cargo.lock` diffs (on 2026-11-15 `rmcp` 3.10.0 was about to pass the cooldown and comes in this way, since rmcp-server-kit requires `rmcp = "3"`). (3) Write every crates.io requirement as the full locked version, as `cargo add` does (for example `hyper = "1.12.0"`, `caps = "0.5.6"`, `serde = "1.0.229"`), until `python3 .omo/reviews/kcc-full-review-2026-11-15/check_dep_reqs.py` exits 0 (it reads `cargo metadata --locked --all-features` for both workspaces). (4) Re-run both dry runs: no package line (`Updating|Adding|Removing|Downgrading <crate> v...`) may remain; the `Updating crates.io index` line is expected. Any remaining direct-dependency `Unchanged ... (available: ...)` line that is not cooldown-held goes back to step 1; list every other remaining `Unchanged` line in the commit body and in the overlay as either too young or held by a named dependent (`cargo tree --invert <crate>@<version>`). (5) Fix every compile, clippy, rustdoc and test break the new versions cause, with no behaviour change, no `#[allow]` and no test expectation change unless an upstream API rename forces it (name it in the commit body). Then re-run every generator and require a zero diff, or commit the regenerated output and name it in the commit body: `cargo xtask catalog build --version 5.1` (and 5.0, 4.3; in the main checkout with `catalog/.cache`), `cargo xtask i18n-gen`, `cargo xtask gen-tool-docs`, `cargo xtask gen-metric-docs`, and the seccomp profile by todo 70's two-trace procedure (image build, `/kcc` copied to `${CARGO_TARGET_DIR:-target}/kcc-musl`, two `cargo xtask seccomp trace` runs merged into `<target>/seccomp-trace-union`, `cargo xtask seccomp gen --traces <target>/seccomp-trace-union`); commit the regenerated profile and name every added and every removed syscall in the commit body (the preceding `gen --check --traces ...` lists them; it exits non-zero on a difference, so do not chain it); if an updated dependency brings a syscall that `gen` reports as unmapped, assign it a group in `xtask/src/seccomp_groups.rs` and name that xtask change in the commit body; step 10's container e2e under that profile must pass. (6) `cargo deny check` and `cargo deny --manifest-path xtask/Cargo.toml check`: remove `skip` entries in `deny.toml` and their mirror in `clippy.toml` `allowed-duplicate-crates` that no longer apply, and keep each remaining reason accurate, version numbers included, as well as the version-bearing comments at `deny.toml:26` and `:29-30`; add a skip only when no aligned version of the duplicate exists, with a reason naming both dependents. (7) Tools installed as crates: find every pin with `grep -nE 'tool: (cargo-|taplo@)' .github/workflows/*.yml` (the gate tools in `ci.yml`, plus the build tool `cargo-zigbuild` in `release.yml`) and bump each, plus the `ci.yml:27-28` header comment and the overlay's "The project" list and deviation 2, to its latest version past the cooldown. `cargo install` ignores the cooldown, so read each version's publish time from `curl -s -A kcc-dev https://crates.io/api/v1/crates/<crate>/versions` (`created_at`) and take the newest version that is not yanked and is at least 3 days old; the taplo crate is `taplo-cli`. On 2026-11-15 that meant cargo-deny 0.19.9 -> 0.20.2 and cargo-audit 0.22.2 -> 0.23.0; cargo-vet 0.10.3, cargo-machete 0.9.2, taplo-cli 0.10.0 and cargo-zigbuild 0.24.1 were already the latest. Install the same versions locally with `cargo install <crate>@<version> --locked` before running G1, and adapt `deny.toml` and `.cargo/audit.toml` to any configuration change in those releases (read their changelogs). Outside D-LATEST: shellcheck, actionlint, the action SHAs and the toolchains (`1.100.0` is the latest stable per `rustup check`; the dated rustfmt nightly stays pinned). (8) `cargo vet` (D-VET, with network) in both workspaces, following todo 71's procedure: versions covered by an imported audit set need nothing; for each other crate add a targeted `cargo vet add-exemption <crate> <version> --criteria safe-to-deploy` named in the commit body; never blanket `cargo vet regenerate exemptions`, never `cargo vet certify`; then `cargo vet prune`, and commit both `imports.lock` files so `cargo vet --locked` passes as in CI. (9) Every selected version must build on the MSRV `1.100.0`, the latest stable; if the Rust-version-aware resolver falls back to an older version for that reason, record the crate and the version it needs. (10) Container check: new versions can add syscalls that the seccomp allowlist kills, and the container workflow's drift check does not run without a push, so run `bash deploy/podman/e2e.sh --container --image kcc:test` against the image built in step 5 (rebuild it with `podman build --format docker -t kcc:test .` if needed; never with `KCC_LIVE_*`); it must pass; afterwards remove that image with `podman rmi kcc:test` and nothing else. (11) In the project overlay mark deviation 3 resolved with the date and the held-back list from step 4, and add a CHANGELOG `Unreleased` line naming the major updates. (No review finding; the scope is stated above.)

New transitive crates brought in by an updated version (for example `sponge-cursor`) are expected and are named in the commit body.

## Final verification wave
> Runs in parallel after ALL todos. ALL must APPROVE. Surface results and wait for the user's explicit okay before declaring complete.

> **Convergence rule.**
> 1. A finding becomes a new `- [ ] N.` row only when it gives a reproduction and is one of these:
>    - (a) a secret reaching a principal without `RevealSecrets`, or reaching a log, audit entry, error or Debug output;
>    - (b) router lockout, a wrong router command, data loss, or a lost audit entry while the writer lives;
>    - (c) a violated Must have, Must NOT have, IS row or owner decision (D-H1, D-H7, D-INFO, D-DELIVERY, D-NODEPLOY, D-VET, D-LATEST);
>    - (d) a reproducible failure of a main flow;
>    - (e) a G0/G1 failure;
>    - (f) a validated review finding that is not actually closed (a ledger row without a real fix);
>    - (g) a behaviour change versus `349b7fb` that no todo lists.
> 2. Everything else goes to `docs/BACKLOG.md` in plain language. A dependency version that passed the cooldown after todo 75 ran is not a finding.
> 3. At most two finding rounds. The second round's blocker list is frozen.
> 4. One confirmation round (G1 plus the frozen rows' proving scenarios), then stop and report to the owner.

- [ ] F1. Plan compliance audit
  Recommended task executor category: unspecified-high.
  - For every todo 1-75, confirm that each instruction and each listed finding fix exists in code (file:line).
  - Confirm the Must NOTs hold:
    - `cargo xtask allow-gate` passes;
    - `git diff 349b7fb -- '**/Cargo.toml'` adds no `[workspace.dependencies]` entry and no crate that was not in `[workspace.dependencies]` at `349b7fb` (members may add existing workspace dependencies; version changes come only from todos 71 and 75);
    - the audit format is unchanged;
    - the vendored guideline files are unchanged (`sha256sum -c`).
  - Run `python3 .omo/reviews/kcc-full-review-2026-11-15/check_coverage.py docs/review-ledger-2026-11-15.md`; it must exit 0.
  - Spot-check at least 40 ledger rows by reading the commit diff against the finding text: all highs, all mediums of waves 1-4, and 10 random lows/infos.
  - Every todo's evidence file exists, with red and green runs.
  - Output `.omo/evidence/F1-kcc-review-fixes.md`, APPROVE/REJECT per todo.
- [ ] F2. Code quality review
  Recommended task executor category: unspecified-high.
  - Run gate G1 in full on a clean checkout (`cargo clean` first; target dir on `/`; gate tools installed at the `ci.yml` pins) and capture `.omo/evidence/F2-kcc-review-fixes.log`.
  - Review the diff from `349b7fb` against the vendored core checklist (§11), the http-services and mcp-servers checklists and the project overlay. Specifically:
    - no new blocking calls in async fns;
    - cancel-safety comments are correct;
    - no new `|safe` on `String`;
    - one masker, one CLI-word rule and one confirmation rule (grep for re-introduced copies);
    - record-typed AAD is enforced on every seal/open site, with no legacy fallback for the associated data (the existing plaintext-to-sealed path `CredentialStore::migrate`, `credentials.rs:241`, is unrelated and stays).
  - Run `cargo mutants` on `crates/kcc-config/src/mask.rs`, the audit writer and the bracket. Install it with `cargo install cargo-mutants --locked` if missing; if that's impossible, record a non-blocking note.
  - APPROVE only if G1 is fully green.
- [ ] F3. Real manual QA
  Recommended task executor category: unspecified-high (load skills `playwright`, `visual-qa`).
  - Start `kcc-fake-router` (router mode and `--mode extender`) and `kcc serve`.
  - Verify with Playwright and curl:
    - In the explorer, for `show running-config` and `show interface`, a viewer sees the Web marker `********` and never an ns3 blob; an admin with RevealSecrets sees the blob.
    - MCP `kcc_rci_read` with a viewer key returns redacted text.
    - A login with a 1 MiB username is refused, and `audit.jsonl` grows by at most one bounded entry.
    - Clearing a firewall rule's description on a disabled rule keeps the rule disabled.
    - After `kcc apikey revoke`, the key fails within 5 s. After `kcc user disable`, that user's Web session ends within 5 s.
    - A migration with both a password change and a management IP change completes on the fake.
    - A management change on the Web always shows the dialog.
    - Behind `public_url = https://...` with `trusted_proxies`, the cookie has `Secure` and `__Host-`.
    - Logs forward to the fake sinks, and recover after a sink outage.
  - Save screenshots as `.omo/evidence/F3-<area>.png`.
  - Classify defects by the convergence rule.
- [ ] F4. Ideal-state fidelity
  Recommended task executor category: unspecified-high.
  - For each row IS-1..IS-14, re-run the proving scenario named in `## Success criteria`. Judge IS-13's dependency part against todo 75's evidence: a version published or past the cooldown after todo 75's commit is not a FAIL.
  - Record PASS/FAIL with the evidence path in `.omo/evidence/F4-kcc-review-fixes.md`.
  - Classify a FAIL by the convergence rule.

## Commit strategy
- **First commit, before todo 1:** `docs(plan): add the review fix plan and review evidence`. It contains `.omo/plans/kcc-review-fixes.md`, `.omo/drafts/kcc-review-fixes.md` and `.omo/reviews/kcc-full-review-2026-11-15/`, exactly as reviewed, with no edits.
- **Then one commit per todo**, using its Commit line (Conventional Commits with crate scopes):
  - on `master`; no push, no force, no history rewrite;
  - with the repo-local git identity as configured (andrico21, noreply); never change git config.
- **Each commit leaves G0 green.** A commit that changes an existing test's expectation because a finding is fixed names that test in its body.
- **Generated artifacts** are committed with the change that produced them.
- **The finding ledger** (todo 74) is committed as `docs/review-ledger-2026-11-15.md` and records, for every finding id, the commit that closed it.
- **Landing a worktree todo:** rebase the todo's branch onto `master`, re-run G0 on the rebased tree, then fast-forward `master` to that single commit.

## Success criteria
> One row per IS row. The plan is complete only when every IS row has a delivering todo and a proving QA scenario; F4 checks the delivered behavior against these rows 1:1.
